- **mtu-blackhole-hunt**: Find a path MTU problem: "small requests work but large ones hang", "connection stalls after the TLS ClientHello", "only breaks over the VPN or in the overlay network", "ssh logs in but hangs on ls". The signature is a completed handshake and then large packets vanishing because ICMP fragmentation-needed is filtered, so path MTU discovery fails. Prove it with ping -M do -s N binary-searched to the largest payload that passes, tracepath, and a capture showing full-size segments retransmitted and never acknowledged.
